Step 1. Generate API Key\n\n::generate-api-key\n\n## Step 2. Pull and Run the NIM\n\n```bash\n$ docker login nvcr.io\nUsername: $oauthtoken\nPassword: \u003cPASTE_API_KEY_HERE\u003e\n```\n\nPull and run the NVIDIA NIM with the command below. This will download the optimized model for your infrastructure.\n\n```bash\nexport NGC_API_KEY=\u003cPASTE_API_KEY_HERE\u003e\nexport LOCAL_NIM_CACHE=~/.cache/nim\nmkdir -p \"$LOCAL_NIM_CACHE\"\nchmod -R a+w \"$LOCAL_NIM_CACHE\"\ndocker run -it --rm \\\n    --gpus all \\\n    --shm-size=16GB \\\n    -e NGC_API_KEY \\\n    -v \"$LOCAL_NIM_CACHE:/opt/nim/.cache\" \\\n    -p 8000:8000 \\\n    nvcr.io/nim/nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b:latest\n```\n\n## Step 3. Test the NIM\n\nYou can now make a local API call using this curl command:\n\n```bash\ncurl -X 'POST' \\\n'http://0.0.0.0:8000/v1/chat/completions' \\\n-H 'accept: application/json' \\\n-H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\\n-d '{\n    \"model\": \"nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b\",\n    \"messages\": [{\"role\":\"user\", \"content\":\"Which number is larger, 9.11 or 9.8?\"}],\n    \"max_tokens\": 64\n}'\n```\n\nFor more details on getting started with this NIM, visit the [NVIDIA NIM Docs](https://docs.nvidia.com/nim/large-language-models/latest/getting-started.html).5a:[\"$\",\"$L5c\",null,{\"spec\":{\"openAPISpec\":{\"openapi\":\"3.1.0\",\"info\":{\"title\":\"NVIDIA NIM API for nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b\",\"description\":\"The NVIDIA NIM REST API. class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]">NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B</h1>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="model-summary">Model Summary</h2>













































<div class="max-w-full overflow-x-auto"><table class="my-0! whitespace-nowrap" tabindex="0"><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left"></th><th style="text-align:left"></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Total Parameters</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">120B (12B active)</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Architecture</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">LatentMoE - Mamba-2 + MoE + Attention hybrid with Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Context Length</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">Up to 1M tokens</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Minimum GPU Requirement</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">8× H100-80GB</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Supported Languages</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">English, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Spanish, Chinese</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Best For</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">Agentic workflows, long-context reasoning, high-volume workloads (e.g. IT ticket automation), tool use, RAG</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Reasoning Mode</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">Configurable on/off via chat template (<code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">enable_thinking=True/False</code>)</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>License</strong></td><td style="text-align:left"><a target="_blank" href="https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/agreements/enterprise-software/nvidia-nemotron-open-model-license/">NVIDIA Nemotron Open Model License</a></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Release Date</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">March 11, 2026</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="quick-start">Quick Start</h2>
<blockquote>
<p>Use <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">temperature=1.0</code> and <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">top_p=0.95</code> across <strong>all tasks and serving backends</strong> — reasoning, tool calling, and general chat alike.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>For more details on how to deploy and use the model - see the <a target="_blank" href="#quick-start-guide">Quick Start Guide</a> below!</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="model-overview">Model Overview</h2>
<p><strong>Model Developer:</strong> NVIDIA Corporation</p>
<p><strong>Model Dates:</strong> December 2025 - March 2026</p>
<p><strong>Data Freshness:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>The post-training data has a cutoff date of February 2026.</li>
<li>The pre-training data has a cutoff date of June 2025.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="what-is-nemotron">What is Nemotron?</h3>
<p>NVIDIA Nemotron™ is a family of open models with open weights, training data, and recipes, delivering leading efficiency and accuracy for building specialized AI agents.</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="description">Description</h2>
<p><strong>Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16</strong> is a large language model (LLM) trained by NVIDIA, designed to deliver strong agentic, reasoning, and conversational capabilities. It is optimized for collaborative agents and high-volume workloads such as IT ticket automation. Like other models in the family, it responds to user queries and tasks by first generating a reasoning trace and then concluding with a final response. The model&#x27;s reasoning capabilities can be configured through a flag in the chat template.</p>
<p>The model employs a hybrid <strong>Latent Mixture-of-Experts (LatentMoE)</strong> architecture, utilizing interleaved Mamba-2 and MoE layers, along with select Attention layers. Distinct from the Nano model, the Super model incorporates <strong>Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)</strong> layers for faster text generation and improved quality, and it is trained using <strong>NVFP4</strong> quantization to maximize compute efficiency. The model has <strong>12B active parameters</strong> and <strong>120B parameters in total</strong>.</p>
<p>The supported languages include: English, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Spanish, and Chinese</p>
<p>This model is ready for commercial use.</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="licenseterms-of-use">License/Terms of Use</h2>
<p><strong>GOVERNING TERMS</strong>: The trial service is governed by the <a target="_blank" href="https://assets.ngc.nvidia.com/products/api-catalog/legal/NVIDIA%20API%20Trial%20Terms%20of%20Service.pdf">NVIDIA API Trial Terms of Service</a>. Use of this model is governed by the <a target="_blank" href="https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/agreements/enterprise-software/nvidia-nemotron-open-model-license/">NVIDIA Nemotron Open Model License</a>.</p>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="benchmarks">Benchmarks</h3>

<p>All evaluation results were collected via <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Evaluator">Nemo Evaluator SDK</a> and for most benchmarks, the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Skills">Nemo Skills Harness</a>. For reproducibility purposes, more details on the evaluation settings can be found in the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Evaluator/tree/main/packages/nemo-evaluator-launcher/examples/nemotron/nemotron-3-super">Nemo Evaluator SDK configs folder</a> and the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Evaluator/blob/main/packages/nemo-evaluator-launcher/examples/nemotron/nemotron-3-super/reproducibility.md">reproducibility tutorial for Nemotron 3 Super</a>. The open source container on Nemo Skills packaged via NVIDIA&#x27;s Nemo Evaluator SDK used for evaluations can be found <a target="_blank" href="https://catalog.ngc.nvidia.com/orgs/nvidia/teams/eval-factory/containers/nemo_skills">here</a>. In addition to Nemo Skills, the evaluations also used dedicated open-source packaged containers for Tau-2 Bench (default prompt), Terminal Bench Hard (48 tasks), ScaleAI Multi Challenge Multi-turn Instruction Following, and Ruler.</p>
<p>The following benchmarks are not onboarded yet in our open source tools and for these we used either their official open source implementation or otherwise an internal scaffolding that we plan to open source in the future: SWE Bench Verified (OpenHands), SWE Bench Multilingual (OpenHands), BrowseComp with Search (internal implementation with Serp API), Terminal Bench Core 2.0 (Harbor).</p>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="deployment-geography-global">Deployment Geography: Global</h3>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="use-case">Use Case</h3>
<p>NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16 is a general purpose reasoning and chat model intended to be used in English, Code, and supported multilingual contexts. This model is optimized for collaborative agents and high-volume workloads. It is intended to be used by developers designing AI Agent systems, chatbots, RAG systems, and other AI-powered applications. This model is also suitable for complex instruction-following tasks and long-context reasoning.</p>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="release-date">Release Date</h3>
<p>Hugging Face - 03/11/2026 via <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16">Hugging Face</a></p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="references">Reference(s)</h2>
<ul>
<li><a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/nvidia-nemotron-v3">NVIDIA Nemotron 3 model family on Hugging Face</a></li>
<li><a target="_blank" href="https://research.nvidia.com/labs/nemotron/files/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-Technical-Report.pdf">NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Super Technical Report</a></li>
</ul>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="model-architecture">Model Architecture</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Architecture Type:</strong> Mamba2-Transformer Hybrid Latent Mixture of Experts (LatentMoE) with Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)</li>
<li><strong>Network Architecture:</strong> Nemotron Hybrid LatentMoE</li>
<li><strong>Number of model parameters:</strong> 120B Total / 12B Active</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="model-design">Model Design</h2>
<p>The model utilizes the <strong>LatentMoE</strong> architecture, where tokens are projected into a smaller latent dimension for expert routing and computation, improving accuracy per byte. The Super model is pre-trained using NVFP4 quantization — the first model in the Nemotron 3 family trained at this precision. The majority of linear layers use NVFP4 for weights, activations, and gradients, while select layers (including latent projections, MTP layers, QKV/attention projections, and embeddings) are maintained in BF16 or MXFP8 for training stability. The model includes <strong>Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)</strong> layers using a shared-weight design across prediction heads. This improves training signal quality, enables faster inference via native speculative decoding, and supports more stable autoregressive drafting at longer draft lengths compared to independently trained offset heads.</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="training-methodology">Training Methodology</h2>
<p>Stage 1: Pre-Training</p>
<ul>
<li><a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base-BF16">NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base-BF16</a> model was pre-trained for over 25T tokens using crawled and synthetic code, math, science, and general knowledge data. Training leveraged NVFP4 quantization for efficiency. All datasets are disclosed in the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16#training-and-evaluation-datasets">Training and Evaluation Datasets</a> section of this document. Major portions of the pre-training corpus are released in the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/nemotron-pre-training-datasets">Nemotron-Pre-Training-Datasets</a> collection.</li>
<li>Software used for pre-training: <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM">Megatron-LM</a></li>
</ul>
<p>Stage 2: Supervised Fine-Tuning</p>
<ul>
<li>The model was further fine-tuned on synthetic code, math, science, tool calling, instruction following, structured outputs, and general knowledge data. This stage incorporated data designed to support long-range retrieval and multi-document aggregation. All datasets are disclosed in the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16#training-and-evaluation-datasets">Training and Evaluation Datasets</a> section of this document. Major portions of the fine-tuning corpus are released in the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/nemotron-post-training-v3">Nemotron-Post-Training-v3</a> collection. <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/DataDesigner">Data Designer</a> is one of the libraries used to prepare these corpora.</li>
</ul>
<p>Stage 3: Reinforcement Learning</p>
<ul>
<li>The model underwent multi-environment reinforcement learning using asynchronous GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) across math, code, science, instruction following, multi-step tool use, multi-turn conversations, and structured output environments. It utilized an asynchronous RL architecture that fully decouples training from inference across separate GPU devices, leveraging in-flight weight updates and MTP to accelerate rollout generation. Conversational quality was further refined through RLHF. All datasets are disclosed in the <em>Training and Evaluation Datasets</em> section of this document. The RL environments and datasets are released as part of <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Gym">NeMo Gym</a>.</li>
<li>Software used for reinforcement learning: <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/RL">NeMo RL</a>, <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Gym">NeMo Gym</a></li>
</ul>
<p>NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16 model is a result of the above work.</p>
<p>The end-to-end training recipe is available in the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Nemotron">NVIDIA Nemotron Developer Repository</a>. Evaluation results can be replicated using the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Evaluator">NeMo Evaluator SDK</a>. <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/DataDesigner">Data Designer</a> is one of the libraries used to prepare the pre and post training datasets. More details on the datasets and synthetic data generation methods can be found in the technical report <a target="_blank" href="https://research.nvidia.com/labs/nemotron/files/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-Technical-Report.pdf">NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Super Technical Report</a>.</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="input">Input</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Input Type(s):</strong> Text</li>
<li><strong>Input Format(s):</strong> String</li>
<li><strong>Input Parameters:</strong> One-Dimensional (1D): Sequences</li>
<li><strong>Other Properties Related to Input:</strong> Maximum context length up to 1M tokens. Supported languages include: English, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Spanish, and Chinese</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="output">Output</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Output Type(s):</strong> Text</li>
<li><strong>Output Format:</strong> String</li>
<li><strong>Output Parameters:</strong> One-Dimensional (1D): Sequences</li>
<li><strong>Other Properties Related to Output:</strong> Maximum context length up to 1M tokens</li>
</ul>
<p>Our AI models are designed and optimized to run on NVIDIA GPU-accelerated systems. By leveraging NVIDIA&#x27;s hardware (e.g. GPU cores) and software frameworks (e.g., CUDA libraries), the model achieves faster training and inference times compared to CPU-only solutions.</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="software-integration">Software Integration</h2>
<ul>
<li>Runtime Engine(s): NeMo 25.11.01</li>
<li>Supported Hardware Microarchitecture Compatibility: NVIDIA Ampere - A100; NVIDIA Blackwell; NVIDIA Hopper - H100-80GB</li>
<li>Operating System(s): Linux</li>
</ul>
<p>The integration of foundation and fine-tuned models into AI systems requires additional testing using use-case-specific data to ensure safe and effective deployment. Following the V-model methodology, iterative testing and validation at both unit and system levels are essential to mitigate risks, meet technical and functional requirements, and ensure compliance with safety and ethical standards before deployment.</p>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="model-versions">Model Version(s)</h2>
<ul>
<li>v1.0 - GA</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="quick-start-guide">Quick Start Guide</h2>
<p>For each inference backend - we&#x27;ll be using the custom <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">super_v3</code> reasoning parser - which you can obtain by following these instructions:</p>

<p>Context length defaults to 256k above. To use up to 1M, set <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">VLLM_ALLOW_LONG_MAX_MODEL_LEN=1</code> and <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">--max-model-len 1M</code></p>
</blockquote>
<h4>SGLang</h4>
<p>Container:</p>

kv_cache_config:
  enable_block_reuse: false
  mamba_ssm_cache_dtype: float32
moe_config:
  backend: TRTLLM
cuda_graph_config:
  enable_padding: true
  max_batch_size: 256
enable_attention_dp: true
EOF</span>

trtllm-serve <span class="token environment constant">PATH</span>/TO/BF16/CHECKPOINT <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--host</span> <span class="token number">0.0</span>.0.0 <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--port</span> <span class="token number">8123</span> <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--backend</span> pytorch <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--max_batch_size</span> <span class="token number">256</span> <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--tp_size</span> <span class="token number">8</span> <span class="token parameter variable">--ep_size</span> <span class="token number">8</span> <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--max_num_tokens</span> <span class="token number">8576</span> <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--trust_remote_code</span> <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--reasoning_parser</span> nano_v3 <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--tool_parser</span> qwen3_coder <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
  <span class="token parameter variable">--extra_llm_api_options</span> extra-llm-api-config.yml
</code></pre></div></div>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="api-client">API Client</h3>
<p>The examples below use the OpenAI-compatible client and work with any of the serving backends above.</p>
<blockquote>
<p>NOTE: For coding agents add the following to the API call - <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">extra_body={“chat_template_kwargs”: {“force_nonempty_content”: True}</code></p>
</blockquote>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_b7cmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code><span class="token keyword">from</span> openai <span class="token keyword">import</span> OpenAI
client <span class="token operator">=</span> OpenAI<span class="token punctuation">(</span>base_url<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"http://localhost:8000/v1"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> api_key<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"EMPTY"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
MODEL <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string">"nvidia/nemotron-3-super"</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Reasoning ON (default)</strong></p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_b8cmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code>response <span class="token operator">=</span> client<span class="token punctuation">.</span>chat<span class="token punctuation">.</span>completions<span class="token punctuation">.</span>create<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    model<span class="token operator">=</span>MODEL<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    messages<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"user"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"Write a haiku about GPUs"</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">16000</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    temperature<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    top_p<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    extra_body<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"chat_template_kwargs"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"enable_thinking"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token boolean">True</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
<span class="token keyword">print</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>response<span class="token punctuation">.</span>choices<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>message<span class="token punctuation">.</span>content<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>

    model<span class="token operator">=</span>MODEL<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    messages<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"user"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"What is the capital of Japan?"</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">16000</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    temperature<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    top_p<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    extra_body<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"chat_template_kwargs"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"enable_thinking"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token boolean">False</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
<span class="token keyword">print</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>response<span class="token punctuation">.</span>choices<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>message<span class="token punctuation">.</span>content<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Low-effort reasoning</strong></p>
<p>Uses significantly fewer reasoning tokens than full thinking mode. Recommended as a starting point before tuning explicit token budgets.</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_basmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code>response <span class="token operator">=</span> client<span class="token punctuation">.</span>chat<span class="token punctuation">.</span>completions<span class="token punctuation">.</span>create<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    model<span class="token operator">=</span>MODEL<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    messages<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"user"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"What is the capital of Japan?"</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">16000</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    temperature<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    top_p<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    extra_body<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"chat_template_kwargs"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"enable_thinking"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token boolean">True</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"low_effort"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token boolean">True</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
<span class="token keyword">print</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>response<span class="token punctuation">.</span>choices<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>message<span class="token punctuation">.</span>content<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="opencode">OpenCode</h3>
<p><a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://opencode.ai/docs">OpenCode</a> is an AI coding agent that runs in your terminal. It connects to any OpenAI-compatible endpoint, making it compatible with all three serving backends above (vLLM, SGLang, and TRT-LLM).</p>
<p>Create or update your <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">~/.config/opencode/opencode.json</code>:</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_bcsmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="JSON"/>JSON</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token property">"$schema"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"https://opencode.ai/config.json"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token property">"model"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"local/nvidia-nemotron-3-super"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token property">"provider"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="token property">"local"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
            <span class="token property">"npm"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"@ai-sdk/openai-compatible"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"name"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"local_backend"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"options"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
                <span class="token property">"baseURL"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"http://localhost:8000/v1"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
                <span class="token property">"apiKey"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"EMPTY"</span>
            <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"models"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
                <span class="token property">"nvidia-nemotron-3-super"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
                    <span class="token property">"name"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"nvidia/nemotron-3-super"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
                    <span class="token property">"limit"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
                        <span class="token property">"context"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1000000</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
                        <span class="token property">"output"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">32768</span>
                    <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
                <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
            <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token property">"agent"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
        <span class="token property">"build"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
            <span class="token property">"temperature"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"top_p"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"max_tokens"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">32000</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token property">"plan"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
            <span class="token property">"temperature"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"top_p"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token property">"max_tokens"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">32000</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<blockquote>
<p>Update <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">baseURL</code> to match whichever backend you are running. The default port above (<code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">8000</code>) matches the vLLM example; SGLang and TRT-LLM use <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">30000</code> and <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">8123</code> respectively.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>To learn more about other supported agent scaffolds - check out <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Nemotron/tree/main/usage-cookbook/Nemotron-3-Super/OpenScaffoldingResources">this resource</a></p>
<details>
<summary><strong> Advanced: Budget-Controlled Reasoning</strong></summary>
<p>Set a hard token ceiling on the reasoning trace using <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">reasoning_budget</code>. The model will attempt to close the trace at the next newline before the budget is hit; if none is found within 500 tokens it closes abruptly at <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">reasoning_budget + 500</code>.</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_5decmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code><span class="token keyword">from</span> typing <span class="token keyword">import</span> Any<span class="token punctuation">,</span> Dict<span class="token punctuation">,</span> List
<span class="token keyword">import</span> openai
<span class="token keyword">from</span> transformers <span class="token keyword">import</span> AutoTokenizer


<span class="token keyword">class</span> <span class="token class-name">ThinkingBudgetClient</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>
    <span class="token keyword">def</span> <span class="token function">__init__</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>self<span class="token punctuation">,</span> base_url<span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token builtin">str</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> api_key<span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token builtin">str</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> tokenizer_name_or_path<span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token builtin">str</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>
        self<span class="token punctuation">.</span>tokenizer <span class="token operator">=</span> AutoTokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>from_pretrained<span class="token punctuation">(</span>tokenizer_name_or_path<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
        self<span class="token punctuation">.</span>client <span class="token operator">=</span> openai<span class="token punctuation">.</span>OpenAI<span class="token punctuation">(</span>base_url<span class="token operator">=</span>base_url<span class="token punctuation">,</span> api_key<span class="token operator">=</span>api_key<span class="token punctuation">)</span>

    <span class="token keyword">def</span> <span class="token function">chat_completion</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>
        self<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        model<span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token builtin">str</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        messages<span class="token punctuation">:</span> List<span class="token punctuation">[</span>Dict<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token builtin">str</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> Any<span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        reasoning_budget<span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token builtin">int</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token number">512</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        max_tokens<span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token builtin">int</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token number">1024</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token operator">**</span>kwargs<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">)</span> <span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token operator">></span> Dict<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token builtin">str</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> Any<span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>
        <span class="token keyword">assert</span> max_tokens <span class="token operator">></span> reasoning_budget<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span>
            <span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f"reasoning_budget must be less than max_tokens. "</span></span>
            <span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f"Got </span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string"> and </span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>reasoning_budget<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string">"</span></span>
        <span class="token punctuation">)</span>

        <span class="token comment"># Step 1: generate the reasoning trace up to the budget</span>
        response <span class="token operator">=</span> self<span class="token punctuation">.</span>client<span class="token punctuation">.</span>chat<span class="token punctuation">.</span>completions<span class="token punctuation">.</span>create<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
            model<span class="token operator">=</span>model<span class="token punctuation">,</span> messages<span class="token operator">=</span>messages<span class="token punctuation">,</span> max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span>reasoning_budget<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token operator">**</span>kwargs
        <span class="token punctuation">)</span>
        reasoning_content <span class="token operator">=</span> response<span class="token punctuation">.</span>choices<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>message<span class="token punctuation">.</span>content
        <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token string">""</span> <span class="token keyword">not</span> <span class="token keyword">in</span> reasoning_content<span class="token punctuation">:</span>
            reasoning_content <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f"</span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>reasoning_content<span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string">.\n\n\n"</span></span>

        reasoning_tokens_len <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token builtin">len</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>
            self<span class="token punctuation">.</span>tokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>encode<span class="token punctuation">(</span>reasoning_content<span class="token punctuation">,</span> add_special_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">False</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">)</span>
        remaining_tokens <span class="token operator">=</span> max_tokens <span class="token operator">-</span> reasoning_tokens_len
        <span class="token keyword">assert</span> remaining_tokens <span class="token operator">></span> <span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span>
            <span class="token string-interpolation"><span class="token string">f"No tokens remaining for response (</span><span class="token interpolation"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>remaining_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span></span><span class="token string">). "</span></span>
            <span class="token string">"Increase max_tokens or lower reasoning_budget."</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">)</span>

        <span class="token comment"># Step 2: continue from the closed reasoning trace</span>
        messages<span class="token punctuation">.</span>append<span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"assistant"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> reasoning_content<span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
        prompt <span class="token operator">=</span> self<span class="token punctuation">.</span>tokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>apply_chat_template<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
            messages<span class="token punctuation">,</span> tokenize<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">False</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> continue_final_message<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">True</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">)</span>
        response <span class="token operator">=</span> self<span class="token punctuation">.</span>client<span class="token punctuation">.</span>completions<span class="token punctuation">.</span>create<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
            model<span class="token operator">=</span>model<span class="token punctuation">,</span> prompt<span class="token operator">=</span>prompt<span class="token punctuation">,</span> max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span>remaining_tokens<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token operator">**</span>kwargs
        <span class="token punctuation">)</span>

        <span class="token keyword">return</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
            <span class="token string">"reasoning_content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> reasoning_content<span class="token punctuation">.</span>strip<span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>strip<span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">""</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>strip<span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> response<span class="token punctuation">.</span>choices<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>text<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
            <span class="token string">"finish_reason"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> response<span class="token punctuation">.</span>choices<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>finish_reason<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<p><strong>Example usage</strong> (32-token reasoning budget):</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_5lecmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code>client <span class="token operator">=</span> ThinkingBudgetClient<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    base_url<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"http://localhost:8000/v1"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    api_key<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"EMPTY"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    tokenizer_name_or_path<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>

result <span class="token operator">=</span> client<span class="token punctuation">.</span>chat_completion<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    model<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    messages<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">[</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"system"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"You are a helpful assistant. /think"</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
        <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"user"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"What is 2+2?"</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    reasoning_budget<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">32</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    max_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">512</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    temperature<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    top_p<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
<span class="token keyword">print</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>result<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
</details>
<h4>Transformers</h4>
<p>The model has been integrated into 🤗 Transformers since v5.3.0. We recommend using the <a target="_blank" href="https://catalog.ngc.nvidia.com/orgs/nvidia/containers/nemo/tags?version=26.02.nemotron_3_super">Nemotron 3 Super</a> container from the NeMo Framework to ensure all required libraries are available.</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_bfsmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code><span class="token keyword">import</span> torch
<span class="token keyword">from</span> transformers <span class="token keyword">import</span> AutoTokenizer<span class="token punctuation">,</span> AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer <span class="token operator">=</span> AutoTokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>from_pretrained<span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
model <span class="token operator">=</span> AutoModelForCausalLM<span class="token punctuation">.</span>from_pretrained<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    <span class="token string">"nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    torch_dtype<span class="token operator">=</span>torch<span class="token punctuation">.</span>bfloat16<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    device_map<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"auto"</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<p>If your Transformers version is lower than v5.3.0, please add <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">trust_remote_code=True</code> when loading the model:</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_bgsmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code>model <span class="token operator">=</span> AutoModelForCausalLM<span class="token punctuation">.</span>from_pretrained<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    <span class="token string">"nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    torch_dtype<span class="token operator">=</span>torch<span class="token punctuation">.</span>bfloat16<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    device_map<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"auto"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    trust_remote_code<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">True</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<p>Please note that the model supports up to a 1M context size, although the default context size in the Hugging Face configuration is 256k due to higher VRAM requirements.</p>
<p>Here is an example of generating outputs with reasoning enabled (the default):</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_bicmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code>messages <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token string">"role"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"user"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"content"</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> <span class="token string">"Write a haiku about GPUs"</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>

tokenized_chat <span class="token operator">=</span> tokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>apply_chat_template<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    messages<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    tokenize<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">True</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    add_generation_prompt<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">True</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    return_tensors<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"pt"</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>to<span class="token punctuation">(</span>model<span class="token punctuation">.</span>device<span class="token punctuation">)</span>

<span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token keyword">not</span> <span class="token builtin">isinstance</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>tokenized_chat<span class="token punctuation">,</span> torch<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Tensor<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>
    input_ids <span class="token operator">=</span> tokenized_chat<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">"input_ids"</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span>
<span class="token keyword">else</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>
    input_ids <span class="token operator">=</span> tokenized_chat

outputs <span class="token operator">=</span> model<span class="token punctuation">.</span>generate<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    input_ids<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    max_new_tokens<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">50</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    temperature<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">1.0</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    top_p<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token number">0.95</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    eos_token_id<span class="token operator">=</span>tokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>eos_token_id
<span class="token punctuation">)</span>

<span class="token keyword">print</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>tokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>decode<span class="token punctuation">(</span>outputs<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token number">0</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<p>To disable reasoning, add <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">enable_thinking=False</code> to <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">apply_chat_template()</code>. By default, <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">enable_thinking</code> is set to <code class="inline rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack px-1 align-bottom font-mono leading-body text-primary">True</code>.</p>
<div class="flex flex-col rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-manitoulinDarkBlack mb-4 sm:max-h-[400px]"><div class="space-between flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2"><div role="radiogroup" class="nv-segmented-control-root nv-segmented-control-root--size-small"><label class="nv-segmented-control-item"><input class="nv-segmented-control-input" type="radio" name="_R_bjcmmndbsnpfiuniv8lb_" checked="" value="Python"/>Python</label></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1"><button aria-label="Copy" data-nvtrack="Get Code/Key" data-nvtrack-adobe-type="modelEngagementClick" class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center font-sans leading-text font-bold flex-row btn-brand btn-sm btn-rounded btn-inverse border-0"><svg data-icon-name="copy-generic" display="inline-block" height="1em" width="1em" class="btn-icon"><use href="/assets/icons/3.8.0/line/copy-generic.svg#copy-generic"></use></svg><div class="grid-stack"><span aria-hidden="true" class="aria-hidden:invisible">Copied</span><span aria-hidden="false" class="text-nowrap aria-hidden:invisible">Copy</span></div></button></div></div><div class="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-1.5 *:min-w-0 *:p-3 *:pt-2"><pre class="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-ms leading-body text-primary [&amp;_code]:block break-all not-prose m-0 border-0" data-testid="highlighted-code"><code>tokenized_chat <span class="token operator">=</span> tokenizer<span class="token punctuation">.</span>apply_chat_template<span class="token punctuation">(</span>
    messages<span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    tokenize<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">True</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    enable_thinking<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token boolean">False</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>

    return_tensors<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token string">"pt"</span>
<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span>to<span class="token punctuation">(</span>model<span class="token punctuation">.</span>device<span class="token punctuation">)</span>
</code></pre></div></div>
<h2 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="training-and-evaluation-datasets">Training and Evaluation Datasets</h2>
<h1 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]">Training</h1>
<p><strong>Data Modality:</strong> Text
<strong>The total size:</strong> 15,573,172,908,990 Tokens
<strong>Total number of datasets:</strong> 153
<strong>Dataset partition:</strong> <em>Training [100%], testing [0%], validation [0%]</em>
<strong>Time period for training data collection:</strong> 2013 to February 24, 2026
<strong>Time period for testing data collection:</strong> 2013 to February 24, 2026
<strong>Time period for validation data collection:</strong> 2013 to February 24, 2026
<strong>Data Collection Method by dataset:</strong> Hybrid: Automated, Human, Synthetic
<strong>Labeling Method by dataset:</strong> Hybrid: Automated, Human, Synthetic</p>
<p>NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16 is pre-trained on a large corpus of high-quality curated and synthetically-generated data. It is trained in the English language, as well as 19 other languages and 43 programming languages. Our sources cover a variety of document types such as: webpages, dialogue, articles, and other written materials. The corpus spans domains including legal, math, science, finance, and more. We also include a small portion of question-answering, and alignment style data to improve model accuracy. The model was trained for approximately 25 trillion tokens.</p>
<p>The post-training corpus for NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-BF16 of high-quality curated and synthetically-generated data. Primary languages used for post-training include English, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Spanish, and Chinese.</p>
<p>These datasets, such as FinePDFs, EssentialWeb, HotpotQA, SQuAD, and HelpSteer3, do not collectively or exhaustively represent all demographic groups (and proportionally therein). For instance, these datasets do not contain explicit mentions of demographic classes such as age, gender, or ethnicity in 64-99% of samples, depending on the source. In the subset where such terms are present, document-based datasets (FinePDFs and EssentialWeb) contain representational skews, such as references to &quot;male&quot; outnumbering those to &quot;female&quot;, and mentions of &quot;White&quot; as the most frequent among ethnic identifiers (comprising 43-44% of ethnicity mentions). To mitigate these imbalances, we recommend considering evaluation techniques such as bias audits, fine-tuning with demographically balanced datasets, and mitigation strategies like counterfactual data augmentation to align with the desired model behavior. This evaluation used a 3,000-sample subset per dataset, identified as the optimal threshold for maximizing embedder accuracy.</p>
<p>During post-training, we generate synthetic data by distilling trajectories, solutions, and translations from strong teacher models and agent systems, often grounded in real tasks or documents and aggressively filtered for quality. For math, code, and science, we start from curated problem sets and use open source permissive models such as GPT-OSS-120B to produce step-by-step reasoning traces, candidate solutions, best-of-n selection traces, and verified CUDA kernels. For long-context and science, we build synthetic QA and reasoning data by retrieving passages from long documents, generating MCQ/OpenQA questions and answers, and paraphrasing them into multiple prompt/response formats to ensure diversity. Across all pipelines we stack automated verification—compilers, numerical checks, language identification—to ensure our data is high quality.</p>
<p>For all domains, we apply a unified data filtering pipeline to ensure that only high-quality, license-compliant, and verifiable samples are used for post-training. We first discard malformed examples using structural checks (e.g., missing tool definitions when tool calls are present). We then aggressively filter reasoning traces exhibiting pathological repetition, such as repeated n-grams within a sliding window or across the entire trajectory, which we found to be a strong indicator of malformed or low-quality reasoning. Finally, based on internal audits of synthetically generated datasets, we observed that some teacher models occasionally produce reasoning traces and final responses that implicitly align with specific political entities or promote nationalistic narratives. To mitigate this, we apply targeted keyword- and regex-based filters and remove all trajectories matching such behavior.</p>
<p>Alongside the model, we release our final pre-training and post-training data, as outlined in this section. For ease of analysis, there is a sample set that is ungated. For all remaining code, math and multilingual data, gating and approval is required, and the dataset is permissively licensed for model training purposes.</p>
<p>More details on the datasets and synthetic data generation methods can be found in the technical report <em><a target="_blank" href="https://research.nvidia.com/labs/nemotron/files/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Super-Technical-Report.pdf"><strong><em>NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Super</em></strong></a></em>.</p>
<p>For more information about the datasets used to train this model, please see the <a target="_blank" href="https://developer.download.nvidia.com/assets/nemo/docs/public-summary-of-training-content-for-nvidia-nemotron.pdf">Public Summary of Training Content</a></p>
<details>
<summary><strong>Click to explore the full dataset catalogue used for training</strong></summary>
<h4><strong>Base Pre-Training Corpus (Nemotron 3 Foundation)</strong></h4>
<p>The foundation of the model is trained on the <strong>Nemotron-3-Nano</strong> corpus, comprising the following collections:</p>



































<div class="max-w-full overflow-x-auto"><table class="my-0! whitespace-nowrap" tabindex="0"><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">Dataset Collection</th><th style="text-align:left">Token Counts</th><th style="text-align:left">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Nemotron-CC-v2</strong> &amp; <strong>v2.1</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">9.13T</td><td style="text-align:left">A massive collection of English web data filtered from Common Crawl, including 2.5T+ tokens of new organic, translated, and synthetically rephrased content.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Nemotron-CC-Code-v1</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">427.9B</td><td style="text-align:left">High-quality code tokens extracted from Common Crawl using the Lynx + LLM pipeline to preserve structure and equations.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Nemotron-Pretraining-Code-v1</strong> &amp; <strong>v2</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">1.09T</td><td style="text-align:left">Curated GitHub code references with multi-stage filtering, deduplication, and large-scale synthetic code data.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Nemotron-CC-Math-v1</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">133.3B</td><td style="text-align:left">High-quality math pre-training dataset preserving LaTeX formatting and mathematical structures.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><strong>Nemotron-Pretraining-Specialized-v1</strong></td><td style="text-align:left">336.4B</td><td style="text-align:left">Synthetic datasets targeting specialized domains such as STEM reasoning and scientific coding.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>

<h3 class="scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+64px)] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(var(--content-top,var(--app-bar-height))+24px)]" id="crawled-and-scraped-from-online-sources-by-nvidia"><strong>Crawled and Scraped from Online Sources by NVIDIA</strong></h3>
<p>The English Common Crawl data was downloaded from the Common Crawl Foundation (see their FAQ for details on their crawling) and includes the snapshots CC-MAIN-2013-20 through CC-MAIN-2025-13. The data was subsequently deduplicated and filtered in various ways described in the Nemotron-CC paper. Additionally, we extracted data for fifteen languages from the following three Common Crawl snapshots: CC-MAIN-2024-51, CC-MAIN-2025-08, CC-MAIN-2025-18. The fifteen languages included were Arabic, Chinese, Danish, Dutch, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Polish, Portuguese, Russian, Spanish, Swedish, and Thai. As we did not have reliable multilingual model-based quality classifiers available, we applied just heuristic filtering instead—similar to what we did for lower quality English data in the Nemotron-CC pipeline, but selectively removing some filters for some languages that did not work well. Deduplication was done in the same way as for Nemotron-CC.</p>
<p>The GitHub Crawl was collected using the GitHub REST API and the Amazon S3 API. Each crawl was operated in accordance with the rate limits set by its respective source, either GitHub or S3. We collect raw source code and subsequently remove any having a license which does not exist in our permissive-license set (for additional details, refer to the <a rel="noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20848">technical report</a>).</p>
